外部DeepSeek原记录 thinking
Task: "pick up the orange juice and place it in the basket".

Note: the task-specific memory for t9_s0 doesn't exist. Let me check task-family and global memory. Wait — the t9 in libero_object is not the mug/microwave t9 from libero_10. Different suite. So the memory references are for object_t9. Let me check.

Scene: agentview shows basket on the LEFT side of the image (image-left), and objects in the middle. Wait, careful: "left"/"right" egocentric. But we just need basket.

Actually in agentview_high.png: basket is at image-right (cols ~700-950), rows ~410-650. The orange juice carton is at image-center, cols ~350-430, rows ~480-650.

Wait, but the low-res policy image shows the basket at image-LEFT. Hmm, that's odd. Low-res image (256) shows basket on left side. High-res 1024 shows basket on the right. That's a big discrepancy... 

Actually wait, look again at the policy image: basket at left ~x 20-80, and objects in middle. In high res: basket at right. These are contradictory. Unless... the images got assigned differently. Let me re-read.

The first returned image (b22681) is 256x256 — that's agentview_policy.png probably, showing basket on the left.

The second (9755fd) is 1024 — agentview_high.png showing basket on the right.

Hmm, that's strange. They should be the same view. Unless the policy image is mirrored or a different camera. Actually maybe the policy image is from a different camera config. Hmm.

Actually, wait. Maybe one of these is agentview and the other agentview_high from different step? No, both step 0.

Hmm, maybe the policy image is rendered differently... Actually in many LIBERO setups, agentview.png and agentview_high.png differ in resolution but same view. Let me not panic; I'll verify by back-projecting.

The wrist image (969904) shows orange juice at top-center, chocolate pudding at left-center, butter at right, and salad dressing bottle at bottom (green cap) — plus basket woven texture at far left. Hmm, wrist shows basket at left too.

Hmm, so wrist and policy show basket at left, high-res shows basket at right? That's weird. Unless the wrist camera is rotated relative to agentview.

Actually the wrist camera is mounted on the gripper and looks down; its orientation relative to world depends on the gripper yaw. So wrist orientation can be rotated.

But policy vs high — both agentview. Hmm. Let me reconsider: maybe the first image is actually the "policy" image which is often a resized/cropped version. Cropping shouldn't mirror.

Hmm, actually wait. Let me look more carefully at the low-res image. Objects: at top there's a green-capped bottle (salad dressing) at center-top. Below-left there's a dark box. A ketchup bottle at center-bottom-left, orange juice at center, a dark brown bottle at right... and basket at left.

In high-res: salad dressing green cap at left-center-top (~col 430, row 400), BBQ sauce at left (~col 265, row 620), orange juice box at center (~col 390, row 560), chocolate pudding box at right (~col 575, row 495), ketchup bottle at center-bottom (~col 550, row 640), basket at right (~col 820, row 520).

In low-res: basket at left. Dressing at top center. So the low-res looks horizontally flipped compared to high-res! Basket left vs right, and in the low-res, orange juice is center, dark bottle right...

Actually in the low-res, I see: basket at far left, a dark small box at center (~col 105, row 120), a green-capped bottle at center-top (~col 150, row 100), a ketchup-ish bottle bottom-center-left (~col 120, row 165), a tall box at center (~col 155, row 150), and a dark bottle at right (~col 195, row 145).

In high-res (flipped): basket far right, dark small box center, dressing at center-left-top, orange juice at center, ketchup at center-bottom, butter at right, BBQ at left.

Hmm. The low-res contains only ~6 objects visible. High res has 7 items (basket, bbq, butter, pudding, ketchup, OJ, dressing) = 6 objects + basket. Low-res: I count basket, dark box, dressing, ketchup, tall box, dark bottle = 6 items? Maybe butter is small/hidden.

So the low-res image seems horizontally mirrored. Odd but it doesn't matter much — I'll use the high-res for pixel picking as instructed, and back_project will give world coords. I'll verify the mapping empirically with one back_project.

Let's plan: pick orange juice box and put in basket.

Localize:
- OJ box in high-res: the yellow/orange carton at cols ~355-430, rows ~480-655. Center ~ (row 570, col 392).
- Basket interior: cols ~700-940, rows ~410-650. Interior opening center ~ (row 520, col 820). Interior appears from ~row 415 (back rim) to ~row 640 (front rim); the cavity interior center maybe (row 540, col 825).

Let me first read the guided memory files. Let me check what exists in task-family dir and global MEMORY.md.

Releasing from z~0.20 while horizontally over the liner avoided the rim contact state and terminated immediately.\n**How to apply:** Identify the container semantically in agentview, use wrist to align over the interior liner/cavity, keep gripper closed during carry, and release from a high over-cavity pose around 4-6 cm above the rim rather than forcing a low descent. For low object-frame basket tasks, z~0.19-0.21 worked here.\n**Falsify:** If a high over-cavity release repeatedly bounces the object out while a lower straight-down placement terminates, this lesson should be narrowed to this basket/box geometry.\n**Related:** [[task-family_libero_object_task_t0]]\n"
